B5322: Agreement:
B5323: For Rel-20 6G ISAC performance evaluation, for object detection and/or tracking, the MDP (missed detection probability) and the FAP type 2 (false alarm probability type 2) are calculated as follows:
B5324: Missed detection probability
B5326: where,
B5327: - is the number of missed targets in the drop n, i.e., the true target not associated with any detected object.
B5328: - is the number of true targets in the drop n.
B5329: - is total number of drops with at least one target per drop.
B5330: FFS: same MDP definition as 5GA ISAC study should also be evaluated
B5331: False alarm probability Type-2
B5333: where,
B5334: - is the number of detected objects but not associated with any true targets in the drop n.
B5335: - is the total number of detected objects in the drop n.
B5336: - is number of drops with at least one detected object.
B5337: FFS: same FAP type-2 definition as 5GA ISAC study should also be evaluated
B5339: Agreement:
B5340: For Rel-20 6G ISAC performance evaluation, for object detection and/or tracking, when data fusion from multiple sensing nodes/pairs is applied:
B5341: Instead of using detected objects per sensing node/pair, the jointly determined object(s) fused from detected objects of multiple sensing nodes/pairs will be used for association between detected object(s) and true target(s), calculating MDP, FAP Type 2 and positioning/velocity accuracy.
B5342: Companies to report the method/algorithm to obtain the jointly fused object(s) from detected objects of multiple sensing nodes/pairs.
B5345: R1-2605743Moderator summary #2 on ISAC evaluationsModerator (CATT)
B5347: Agreement:
B5348: For Rel-20 6G ISAC evaluation on SAC (sensing assisted communication), companies are encouraged to evaluate and report at least one of the following:
B5349: Scope 1: Evaluate performance of sensing measurement/result, which will be used to assist communication;
B5350: Scope 2: Evaluate performance of communication, with and without assistance of sensing measurement/result. The sensing measurement/result to assist communication can be:
B5351: Ideal sensing measurement/result
B5352: Non-ideal sensing measurement/result, and company to report how to model error/offset of non-ideal factors for non-ideal sensing measurement/result
B5353: Real sensing measurement/result, which is obtained from simulation
B5354: Note: companies to report which is used in their simulation.
B5355: For Rel-20 6G ISAC evaluation on SAC, for Scope 2:
B5356: The communication use case(s) in AI 10.8.2 are encouraged
B5357: Companies to report whether the location and/or velocity information of communication UE and/or TRP is needed or not in their SAC algorithm/mechanism
B5358: Note: this does not imply specifying exchange of location and/or velocity information between UE and TRP
B5359: Companies to report the time-frequency resource cost due to sensing for SAC and necessary details on the definition of the time-frequency resource cost based on TR38.765 as starting point
B5360: Comparison with benchmark and benchmark details should also be reported as well as other necessary details if any
B5361: Companies are encouraged to use communication-related performance metrics as agreed in communication agendas
B5362: Companies are encouraged to use baseline/benchmark mechanism as agreed in communication agendas for comparison; Use of state-of-the-art mechanism without assistance of sensing measurement/result for comparison is not precluded
B5363: Companies are encouraged to use additional communication-related evaluation assumptions (if necessary but not discussed/agreed in AI 10.8.1) as agreed in communication agendas
B5364: Note: How to draw conclusion/observation is a separate discussion.
B5366: Agreement:
B5367: For Rel-20 6G ISAC evaluation on SAC, for Scope 1, the following sensing measurement/results and corresponding performance metrics are considered:
B5368: Delay/Doppler/angle/power/phase related information of the ISAC channel
B5369: Performance metric:
B5370: RMSE of delay of the path(s) used to assist communication
B5371: RMSE of Doppler the path(s) used to assist communication
B5372: RMSE of angles (one or more of AOD, ZOD, AOA, ZOA) of the path(s) used to assist communication
B5373: RMSE of power of the path(s) used to assist communication
B5374: FFS: details on the power of the path(s)
B5375: FFS: RMSE of phase of the path(s) used to assist communication
B5376: Companies to report how to select the path(s), how to derive RMSE from multiple paths and how such sensing measurement/result of the paths(s) is able to assist communication 
B5377: Note: when evaluated, companies are not required to report every metric
B5378: Enrivonment (large surface, wall) that reconstructed by sensing
B5379: Performance metric:
B5380: Mean value of absolute perpendicular distance from the sensed specular reflection point to the ground-truth plane
B5381: Mean value of absolute perpendicular distance from the ground truth specular reflection point to the reconstructed plane
B5382: Note: when evaluated, companies are not required to report every metric
B5383: EO type-2 should be modeled in the simulation in this case
B5384: Companies are not required to evaluate and report every sensing measurement/result.
B5385: Note: In Scope 1, the performance metrics for detecting object for the purpose of SAC are the same as those for object detection and/or tracking, and thus already included in Rel-20 6G ISAC object detection and/or tracking study.
B5386: Note 2: How to draw conclusion/observation is a separate discussion.
B5388: Agreement:
B5389: For Rel-20 6G ISAC evaluation on SAC, for evaluation assumptions, the evaluation assumptions of baseline cases/combinations of object detection and/or tracking are reused as starting point. In addition, one or more of the following adjustment may be considered:
B5390: Dropping of sensing target is optional
B5391: In case reconstruction of enrivonment (large surface, wall that modeled by EO type-2) is evaluated, additional sensing capable UEs can be dropped for reconstructing environment
B5392: Other adjustment is not precluded
B5393: Companies should report the adjustments (if any) with necessary details when reporting simulation results.

B5499: Agreement:
B5500: Study on a sensing RS resource consisting of one or multiple component RS. 
B5501: Study the following options for component RS 
B5502: Component RS can be RS for communication is reused for sensing
B5503: Component RS can be enhanced RS for sensing, taking reuse of RS for communication as starting point
B5504: Component RS can be dedicated RS for sensing
B5505: Note: The multiple component RS of a sensing RS resource can be of single or multiple RS types.
B5506: Companies to provide necessity, theoretical analysis, performance evaluation and specification impacts on the above options and the sensing RS resource in next RAN1 meetings. 
B5507: Note: RE pattern of the sensing RS resource is union set of all REs of all component RS
B5508: FFS: details of component RS
B5510: Agreement:
B5511: Study the following two options to generate a sensing RS resource
B5512: Option 1: A sensing RS resource has single antenna port, which can be precoded or non-precoded
B5513: Option 2: A sensing RS resource can have multiple antenna ports, which can be precoded or non-precoded 
B5514: Companies to provide necessity, theoretical analysis, performance evaluation and specification impacts on the above options in next RAN1 meetings.

B5731: Peak-to-Sidelobe Ratio (PSLR), Integrated Sidelobe Ratio (ISLR), and Main lobe width are defined based on AF as follows.
B5732: Definition of power
B5733: Opt 1. Power is defined in delay-Doppler domain 
B5734: Opt 2. Power is defined in delay domain with zero or non-zero Doppler cut 
B5735: Main lobe width is defined as the width of AF measured at the points where the power drops to half (-3 dB) of the peak main lobe power (3dB width). 
B5736: Main lobe region (M) is the region corresponding to the first ‘null-to-null’ region from the main lobe.
B5737: Side lobe region (S) is defined as the entire region except the first ‘null-to-null’ region from the main lobe
